# UAT coverage of acceptance criteria

Generated by `npm run uat:trace` from the implementation plan and the titles of the tests; do not edit by hand. A criterion is covered when a live (not skipped) test title carries its id. A story with the status DONE, DONE_PENDING_ENV or DONE_UNCONFIRMED in `docs/story-status.json` fails the gate when one of its ids is not covered; a DONE_PENDING_ENV or DONE_UNCONFIRMED story may declare the ids that only an external system can prove (field externalChecks).

Releases: R0, R1 (260 stories).
Criteria named in a live test title: 607 of 836 (named only in skipped tests: 0; named nowhere: 229).
Stories with a done status (DONE, DONE_PENDING_ENV, DONE_UNCONFIRMED): 169; their criteria covered: 482 of 502, plus 20 declared external checks.

| AC-ST-CTR-001-2 | ST-CTR-001 | apps/api/test/ctr/contract-register.test.ts: answers a replay with the first body, refuses the same key with another body and a new key with the same code, and leaves one contract and one event (AC-ST-CTR-001-2)<br>packages/db/test/ctr-contract-store.test.ts: refuses the same code in the tenant with CONTRACT_CODE_DUPLICATE but not in another tenant, and leaves exactly one contract (AC-ST-CTR-001-2) | COVERED |

## External checks

Criteria that a DONE_PENDING_ENV or DONE_UNCONFIRMED story declares in the field externalChecks of docs/story-status.json: they need an external system (or the review of a document) and have no live test here.

| ID | story | what only an external system can prove |
| --- | --- | --- |
| AC-ST-DOC-004-1 | ST-DOC-004 | a real CA sandbox with a personal and an organisation certificate (D05), and an independent validator (pdfsig or DSS) |
| AC-ST-DOC-004-2 | ST-DOC-004 | the spike report of a real CA sandbox: DocMDP P=2, RFC 3161 timestamps and OCSP support (docs/spikes/DOC-SPK-01.md says what ran here) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-001-1 | ST-KERNEL-001 | a Keycloak test realm and a physical Android and iOS device (Keychain or Keystore storage, kill mid-redirect) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-002-1 | ST-KERNEL-002 | a PowerSync service on a cloud PostgreSQL with wal_level=logical and the machvon_sync publication (two-tenant sync) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-002-2 | ST-KERNEL-002 | the same PowerSync service: revocation of a membership on a synced device |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-003-1 | ST-KERNEL-003 | a sandbox CA and two validators on a leader-signed, numbered and sealed file (docs/spikes/KERNEL-003.md) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-003-3 | ST-KERNEL-003 | the review of docs/spikes/KERNEL-003.md, which states that the external-upload path is the R1 primary path and lists the missing D05 inputs (a document, not a test) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-006-1 | ST-KERNEL-006 | a Vietnamese cloud account: PITR restore into the second site with a continuous writer (RPO and RTO) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-006-2 | ST-KERNEL-006 | the candidate cloud inventory of a real provider (residency of every resource, backup target and log sink) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-006-3 | ST-KERNEL-006 | a restored database and an object-storage replica to compare (checksums, counts, dangling file references) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-012-2 | ST-KERNEL-012 | a built container image (no Docker on the build machine) inspected for secrets and build arguments |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-012-3 | ST-KERNEL-012 | a staging database password rotation under load |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-040-1 | ST-KERNEL-040 | a running PostgreSQL primary with a standby and a WAL archive at a second site, then a PITR restore |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-040-2 | ST-KERNEL-040 | the restored copy and the verification script against real data |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-041-1 | ST-KERNEL-041 | a versioned, replicated object-storage bucket |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-041-2 | ST-KERNEL-041 | a staging Redis flush while the application runs |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-042-1 | ST-KERNEL-042 | a Keycloak realm export and a PostgreSQL restore in an isolated environment |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-042-3 | ST-KERNEL-042 | a Keycloak instance and the on-call paging channel (10 failed logins paged within 5 minutes) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-044-3 | ST-KERNEL-044 | a Temporal persistence restored from backup (history replay, stuck-workflow alert) |
| AC-ST-KERNEL-050-3 | ST-KERNEL-050 | the telemetry stack of the infrastructure inventory entering through the entry gate (runbook, restore of dashboards and alerts, alert on the collector) |
